    var hasrun = false;

    function ll() {
        if (hasrun) {
            return;
        }
        hasrun = true;
        loadthumbs();
    }

    function performancelistener() {
        this.loadeventend = function(t) {
            settimeout(function(){ ll(); }, 5);
        }
    }

    var t = new performance(new performancelistener()), lltimeout = 15000;

    if (typeof performance === "undefined") {
        lltimeout = 1000;
    }

    settimeout(function(){ ll(); }, lltimeout);

    function loadthumbslazyload() {
        var image = document.queryselectorall('.js-preload'),
            thumbtouse = 'data-mediumthumb';

        for (var i = 0; i < image.length; i++) {
            var itemscount  = image[i];

            if (itemscount.src != itemscount.getattribute(thumbtouse)) {
                itemscount.src = itemscount.getattribute(thumbtouse);
            }

            if (i == 10) {
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    loadthumbslazyload();

    // thumbnail fetching:
    function loadthumbs() {
        var image = document.queryselectorall('.js-preload'),
            thumbtouse = 'data-mediumthumb';
        for (var i = 0; i < image.length; i++) {
            var itemscount = image[i];
            if (itemscount.getattribute(thumbtouse)) {
                if (itemscount.src != itemscount.getattribute(thumbtouse)) {
                    itemscount.src = itemscount.getattribute(thumbtouse);
                }
            }
        }
    }

<script type="text/javascript">
    var captchatype = document.getelementbyid('captcha_type'),
        captchatoken = document.getelementbyid('captcha_token'),
        haspagecheckboxcaptcha = function() { return boolean(document.queryselector('.g-recaptcha:not(.signinmodalcaptcha)')); },
        login_signup_modal_signup = {"activated":false};

    function recaptchav3apicall() {
        grecaptcha.enterprise.ready(function () {
            grecaptcha.enterprise.execute('6lectvslaaaaac51v1_ha5ywopnhcvybjaulh5us', {action: 'signup'}).then(function (token) {
                addingtokens(token);
            });
        });
    }

    function initializev3captcha() {
        var promise = new promise(function (resolve, reject) {
            // check if a captcha token already exists (e.g., from checkbox captcha on the page)
            // if it does, reuse it instead of trying to load a new script with a different site key
            if (captchatoken && captchatoken.value) {
                console.log('reusing existing captcha token');
                resolve('done');
                return;
            }
            
            // critical: check if grecaptcha is already loaded (checkbox captcha on page like cpp)
            // google doesn't support multiple site keys on same page, so skip v3 initialization
            // let signup proceed without separate captcha - user will complete checkbox captcha after signup
            if (
                haspagecheckboxcaptcha()
                && typeof window.grecaptcha === "object"
                && window.grecaptcha.enterprise
                && typeof window.grecaptcha.enterprise.ready === 'function'
            ) {
                console.log('checkbox captcha already loaded on page - skipping v3 captcha for signup modal. user will complete checkbox captcha after signup.');
                // resolve immediately - signup can proceed, user completes checkbox captcha later
                resolve('done');
                return;
            }
            
            // check if grecaptcha.enterprise is fully loaded and ready
            if (typeof window.grecaptcha === "object" && window.grecaptcha.enterprise && typeof window.grecaptcha.enterprise.ready === 'function') {
                // enterprise api is ready, execute immediately
                grecaptcha.enterprise.ready(function () {
                    grecaptcha.enterprise.execute('6lectvslaaaaac51v1_ha5ywopnhcvybjaulh5us', {action: 'submit'}).then(function (token) {
                        addingtokens(token);
                        resolve('done');
                    });
                });
            } else if (typeof window.grecaptcha === "object") {
                // grecaptcha exists but enterprise not ready yet - wait for it with interval check
                var checkinterval = setinterval(function() {
                    if (window.grecaptcha.enterprise && typeof window.grecaptcha.enterprise.ready === 'function') {
                        clearinterval(checkinterval);
                        grecaptcha.enterprise.ready(function () {
                            grecaptcha.enterprise.execute('6lectvslaaaaac51v1_ha5ywopnhcvybjaulh5us', {action: 'submit'}).then(function (token) {
                                addingtokens(token);
                                resolve('done');
                            });
                        });
                    }
                }, 50); // check every 50ms
                
                // timeout after 5 seconds to prevent infinite waiting
                settimeout(function() {
                    clearinterval(checkinterval);
                    console.error('recaptcha enterprise api failed to load within timeout for initializev3captcha');
                    reject('timeout');
                }, 5000);
            } else {
                // grecaptcha doesn't exist at all, load the script
                var head = document.getelementsbytagname('head')[0];
                var script = document.createelement('script');
                script.type = 'text/javascript';
                var scriptloaded = false;
                
                script.onload = function () {
                    scriptloaded = true;
                    grecaptcha.enterprise.ready(function () {
                        grecaptcha.enterprise.execute('6lectvslaaaaac51v1_ha5ywopnhcvybjaulh5us', {action: 'submit'}).then(function (token) {
                            addingtokens(token);
                            resolve('done');
                        }).catch(function(error) {
                            console.error('recaptcha execute failed:', error);
                            reject('execute_failed');
                        });
                    });
                };
                
                script.onerror = function() {
                    console.error('failed to load recaptcha script');
                    reject('script_load_failed');
                };
                
                // timeout protection for script loading
                settimeout(function() {
                    if (!scriptloaded) {
                        console.error('recaptcha script loading timeout');
                        reject('script_load_timeout');
                    }
                }, 10000); // 10 second timeout for script load
                
                script.src = 'https://www.google.com/recaptcha/enterprise.js?render=6lectvslaaaaac51v1_ha5ywopnhcvybjaulh5us';
                head.appendchild(script);
            }
        });
        return promise;
    }

    function addingtokens(token) {
        var captchamultipletokens = document.queryselectorall('.js_captcha_token');
        captchatoken && (captchatoken.value = token);

        captchamultipletokens && [].foreach.call(captchamultipletokens, function (el) {
            el.value = token;
        });
    }
</script>
